<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-3E354580-E360-40DE-9965-73067BBCFACC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out $\dfrac{5}{7}$ of $84$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {5} {7} \ \text {of} \ 84 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5} {7} \times 84 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 84} {7} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
&=  5 \times 12 \\\\
&=  60
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$60$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&=  \dfrac {5} {7} \ \text {of} \ 84 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5} {7} \times 84 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 84} {7} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&=  \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times \cancel {7}} {\cancel {7}} \\\\
&=  5 \times 12 \\\\
&=  60
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$60$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-D051087C-D302-45A1-8E7F-00AC09D3B57D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write down the next two numbers in the sequence.

$5, 11, 23, 47, \boxed{\phantom{11}}, \boxed{\phantom{11}}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR:  Let's find the relation between the consecutive terms of the sequence.


The next number is calculated by multiplying $2$ to the difference. Hence, the sequence would be:

$
\begin{matrix}
&   \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+6)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+12)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+24)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+48)} }
&&  \footnotesize {\maroonC
 {(+96)} }  \\
5,  &&   11,  &&  23,  &&   47, && \ldots, && \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First number}          &= 47 + 48 \\\\
                             &= 95 \\\\
\text{Second number}         &= 95 + 96 \\\\
                             &= 191
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$95$

</div>
<div class='answer'>

$191$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_sequence_find_difference_note

&&  \footnotesize {\rtq_katex_color_info {(+96)} }  \\
5,  &&   11,  &&  23,  &&   47, && \ldots, && \ldots
\end{matrix}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First number}          &= 47 + 48 \\\\
                             &= 95 \\\\
\text{Second number}         &= 95 + 96 \\\\
                             &= 191
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \div 100          &= 3.7 \\\\
\dfrac {a} {100}    &= 3.7 \\\\
a                   &= 3.7 \times 100 \\\\
a                   &= 370
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-91E92D06-A849-497B-A843-4A8E1C0A3EB7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which number is *one hundred* less than *three thousand and sixteen*?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3016 - 100 \\\\
&= 2916
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2916$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3016 - 100 \\\\
&= 2916
\end{aligned}
$

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2916$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-407F2714-A43E-4361-B05E-37CC2E87E623'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Write the missing sign $( = , <$ or $>)$ in the box.

$19 \times 3 \ \boxed{\phantom{11}} \  28 \times 2$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{LHS}   &= 19 \times 3 \\\\
             &= 57 \\\\
\text{RHS}   &= 28 \times 2 \\\\
             &= 56 \\\\
\text{LHS}\  &\boxed{>} \ \text{RHS} \\\\
19 \times 3 \ &\boxed{\green
{>}} \ 28 \times 2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$19 \times 3 \ \quad  > \quad  28 \times 2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{LHS}   &= 19 \times 3 \\\\
             &= 57 \\\\
\text{RHS}   &= 28 \times 2 \\\\
             &= 56 \\\\
\text{LHS}\  &\boxed{>} \ \text{RHS} \\\\
19 \times 3 \ &\boxed{\rtq_katex_color_answer{>}} \ 28 \times 2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$19 \times 3 \ \quad  > \quad  28 \times 2$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-622F8B62-0BE0-42FC-B5A4-E1D1EF0B04E7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The temperature inside Nanook’s igloo is $9 \degree \text{C}$ and the temperature outside is $-12 \degree \text{C}$.

How many degrees warmer is it inside than outside? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &= 9 - (- 12)\\\\
                    &= 9 + 12 \\\\
                    &= 21 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$



</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$21 \degree \text {C}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &= 9 - (- 12)\\\\
                    &= 9 + 12 \\\\
                    &= 21 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$


</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CD5A1E82-56E8-47B2-B92C-58E1FED3F03B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Sherry’s train to Bristol was scheduled to leave at $13{:}40$ and to arrive at $14{:}20$

However, the train left eight minutes late and then took $47 \ \text{minutes}$.

At what time did Sherry arrive?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 13{:}40 + 8 \ \text {minutes} + 47 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 13{:}40 + 55 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 14{:}35
\end{aligned}
$

Sherry arrived at $14{:}35$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14{:}35$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 13{:}40 + 8 \ \text {minutes} + 47 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 13{:}40 + 55 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 14{:}35
\end{aligned}
$

Sherry arrived at $14{:}35$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14{:}35$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2CD8DF37-1EC5-419D-94FD-BB6B498FD8C3'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Lisa thinks of her favourite number.

She multiplies her favourite number by $2$, subtracts $3$ and gets $19$.


What is Lisa’s favourite number?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let Lisa’s favourite number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times a - 3      &= 19 \\\\
2a                  &= 19 + 3 \\\\
2a                  &= 22 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                   &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

Lisa’s favourite number $= 11$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$11$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let Lisa’s favourite number $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
2 \times a - 3      &= 19 \\\\
2a                  &= 19 + 3 \\\\
2a                  &= 22 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times 2} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {11 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                   &= 11
\end{aligned}
$

Lisa’s favourite number $= 11$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$11$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What percentage of the ‘*Salmon pasta*’ is carbohydrate?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {percentage of the carbohydrate in Salmon pasta }              &= \dfrac {15} {10+15+5+2+0.2} \times 100 \\\\
                                                                     &= \dfrac {15} {32.2} \times 100 \\\\
                                                                     &= 0.458 \times 100  \\\\
                                                                     &= 45.8 \\\\
                                                                     &= 45.8 \%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$45.8 \% $

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

                                                                     &= 45.8 \%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$4.25 \times 1.34$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4.25 \times 1.34 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {425 \times 134} {100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{56950} {10000} \\\\
&= 5.6950
\end{aligned}
$



</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5.6950$

</div>
</div>

<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4.25 \times 1.34 \\\\
&=  \dfrac {425 \times 134} {100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac{56950} {10000} \\\\
&= 5.6950
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$56950 \div 4.25$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 56950 \div 4.25 \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950} {4.25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950 \times 100} {425} \\\\
&= 134 \times 100 \\\\
&= 13400
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

</div>
</div>

$

&= \dfrac {56950} {4.25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950 \times 100} {425} \\\\
&= 134 \times 100 \\\\
&= 13400
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$42.5 \times 67$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>


$
\begin{aligned}
&=42.5 \times 67 \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67 \times 2} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 134} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695} {2} \\\\
&= 2847.5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2847.5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>
$
\begin{aligned}
&=42.5 \times 67 \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 67 \times 2} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {425 \times 134} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {56950} {10 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5695} {2} \\\\
&= 2847.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Barbara buys a box containing a selection of three types of biscuit.

There are eight chocolate biscuits.

A third of the other biscuits are custard creams.

There are twelve ginger biscuits. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many custard creams are there?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

Custard biscuit are a third of other biscuits then chocolate biscuits so they are a third of ginger biscuits. Using that,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total custard creams}    &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 12 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                                &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

There are $4$ custard creams.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

Custard biscuit are a third of other biscuits then chocolate biscuits so they are a third of ginger biscuits. Using that,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total custard creams}    &= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 12 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {12} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                                &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

There are $4$ custard creams.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many biscuits are in the box?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total biscuits in the box}    &= 8 + 12 + \dfrac {1} {3} \times 12 \\\\
                                     &= 20 + \dfrac {12} {3} \\\\
                                     &= 20 + \dfrac {4 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                                     &= 20 + \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                                     &= 20+ 4 \\\\
                                     &= 24
\end{aligned}
$

There are $24$ biscuits in the box.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$24$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

                                     &= 20 + \dfrac {12} {3} \\\\
                                     &= 20 + \dfrac {4 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                                     &= 20 + \dfrac {4 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
                                     &= 20+ 4 \\\\
                                     &= 24
\end{aligned}
$

There are $24$ biscuits in the box.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-34C4A0D4-9A04-4290-879F-66A94B759004'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In a box of shapes there are three times as many squares as there are circles.

There are twice as many triangles as squares.


If there are $45$ squares, how many shapes are there altogether?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

Number of circles $= \dfrac {45} {3}$.

Number of triangles $= 45 \times 2$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total shapes}    &= 45 + \dfrac {45} {3} + 45 \times 2 \\\\
                        &= 45 + \dfrac {15 \times 3} {3} +90 \\\\
                        &= 45 + \dfrac {15 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} +90 \\\\
                        &= 45 + 15 + 90 \\\\
                        &= 60 + 90 \\\\
                        &= 150
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$150$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

Number of circles $= \dfrac {45} {3}$.

Number of triangles $= 45 \times 2$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total shapes}    &= 45 + \dfrac {45} {3} + 45 \times 2 \\\\
                        &= 45 + \dfrac {15 \times 3} {3} +90 \\\\
                        &= 45 + \dfrac {15 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} +90 \\\\
                        &= 45 + 15 + 90 \\\\
                        &= 60 + 90 \\\\
                        &= 150
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$150$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-C91E6035-9D47-42CB-834A-DB0B8A45E8A6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which bus takes the shortest time from Elgin to Inverness and by how many minutes?

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken by Bus P}          &= 1.06 - 11.47 \\\\
                                    &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 19 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Time taken by Bus Q}          &= 1.50 - 12.33 \\\\
                                    &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 17 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Difference in time}           &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 19 \ \text{minutes} - 1 \ \text{hour} \ 17 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 2 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

Bus Q takes $2 \ \text{minutes}$ less.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

                                    &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 17 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Difference in time}           &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 19 \ \text{minutes} - 1 \ \text{hour} \ 17 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                    &= 2 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

Bus Q takes $2 \ \text{minutes}$ less.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-ECB9BF93-8E27-42A0-873B-87BFA9D37BB8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Janet’s marks on five mental arithmetic tests are:

$15 \quad 19 \quad 13 \quad 18 \quad 20$

What is her mean (average) mark?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Average marks}            &= \dfrac {15 + 19 + 13 + 18 + 20} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {17 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                &= 17
\end{aligned}
$

Janet’s average marks are $= 17$.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$17$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Average marks}            &= \dfrac {15 + 19 + 13 + 18 + 20} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {85} {5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {17 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                                &= 17
\end{aligned}
$

Janet’s average marks are $= 17$.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$17$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-5386A862-7004-4EB7-85BF-4DB8111B2763'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Which is more likely, rolling a $3$ with an unbiased die with six faces, or getting a head with a fair coin?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's calculate the first value.

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Probability}  &= \dfrac { \text{ Favourable Outcomes} } { \text{Total Outcomes} }
 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {6}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's calculate the second value.

$
\begin{aligned} 
\text{Probability}  &= \dfrac { \text{ Favourable Outcomes} } { \text{Total Outcomes} }
 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \times 3} {2 \times 3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {6}
\end{aligned}
$

Using the calculation above $\dfrac {3} {6} > \dfrac {1} {6}$.

So it is more likely to get a head with a fair coin than rolling a $3$ with an unbiased die with six faces.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\text {Getting a head with a fair coin}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's calculate the first value.

$
\begin{aligned} 
rtq_abbr_katex_formula_aligned_probability \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {6}
\end{aligned}
$

Let's calculate the second value.

$
\begin{aligned} 
rtq_abbr_katex_formula_aligned_probability \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {1 \times 3} {2 \times 3} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {3} {6}
\end{aligned}
$

Using the calculation above $\dfrac {3} {6} > \dfrac {1} {6}$.

So it is more likely to get a head with a fair coin than rolling a $3$ with an unbiased die with six faces.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\text {Getting a head with a fair coin}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2EAFF991-0339-4DCB-B936-D87259A62353'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Penny places $10 \text{p}$ coins, touching, in a straight line.

She hopes to make a line of coins that measures $1 \ \text{km}$.

A $10 \text{p}$ coin has a diameter of $25 \ \text{mm}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How long, in metres, is a line of forty $10 \text{p}$ coins?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 40 \times 25 \ \text {mm} \\\\
&= 40 \times \dfrac {25} {1000} \ \text {m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 25} {1000} \ \text {m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 25} {40 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} {2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} \\\\
&= 1 \ \text {m}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1 \ \text {m}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 40 \times 25 \ \text {mm} \\\\
&= 40 \times \dfrac {25} {1000} \ \text {m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 25} {1000} \ \text {m} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 25} {40 \times 25} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} {2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} \\\\
&= 1 \ \text {m}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$1 \ \text {m}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the total value, in pounds, of forty $10 \text{p}$ coins?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 40 \times 10 \ \text {p} \\\\
&= 40 \times \pounds \dfrac {10} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 10} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} {2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} \\\\
&= \pounds 4
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 40 \times 10 \ \text {p} \\\\
&= 40 \times \pounds \dfrac {10} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40 \times 10} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} {2 \times 5 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} {\cancel 2 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel 5} \\\\
&= \pounds 4
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 4$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many coins will Penny need for a $1 \ \text{kilometre}$ line of $10 \text{p}$ coins?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Penny required}  &= \dfrac {1 \ \text {km}} {25 \ \text {mm}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \times 1000000 \ \text {mm}} {25 \ \text {mm}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40000 \times 5 \times 5} {5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40000 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 5} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 5} \\\\
&= 40000
\end {aligned}
$

&= \dfrac {40000 \times 5 \times 5} {5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {40000 \times \cancel 5 \times \cancel 5} {\cancel 5 \times \cancel 5} \\\\
&= 40000
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

What is Joanna’s age, **on $1 \text{st}$ January**, in $2016$?

\text {Joanna’s age on} \ 1 \ {st January}	&= 1 \ \text {st January} \ 2016 - 19 \ \text {August} \ 2004 \\\\

\end {aligned}
$

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6ABD9083-9950-424C-A91E-55291150A4DE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Six girls took a maths test.

Their marks were $13 \quad 18 \quad 14 \quad 20 \quad 7 \quad 18$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the difference between the highest and lowest marks?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Highest marks}    &= 20 \\\\
\text{Lowest marks}     &= 7 \\\\
\text{Difference}       &= 20 - 7 \\\\
                        &= 13
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Highest marks}    &= 20 \\\\
\text{Lowest marks}     &= 7 \\\\
\text{Difference}       &= 20 - 7 \\\\
                        &= 13
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$13$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Ashleigh’s mark was seven more than Bella’s mark and six less than Connie’s mark.

What was Ashleigh’s mark?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

This can be inferred by using trial and error.

Marks $7$ and $20$ meet the criteria. Let's verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Bella’s marks}       &= 7 \\\\
\text {Ashleigh’s marks}    &= 7 + 7 \\\\
                            &= 14 \\\\
\text {Connie’s marks}      &= 14 + 6 \\\\
                            &= 20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>This can be inferred by using trial and error.

Marks $7$ and $20$ meet the criteria. Let's verify.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Bella’s marks}       &= 7 \\\\
\text {Ashleigh’s marks}    &= 7 + 7 \\\\
                            &= 14 \\\\
\text {Connie’s marks}      &= 14 + 6 \\\\
                            &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>

Amira spends five minutes at the shop before walking another twenty-three minutes to get to school.

At what time does Amira reach school?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 7{:}40 + 5 \ \text {minutes} + 23 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 7{:}40 + 28 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 8{:}08 \ \text {a.m.}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$8{:}08 \ \text {a.m.}$

&= 7{:}40 + 28 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 8{:}08 \ \text {a.m.}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What percentage of the girls play in the hockey team but not in the netball team?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Percentage of girls}                  &=  \dfrac {7} {25} \times 100 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {7 \times 100} {25} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {7 \times 4 \times 25} {25} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {7 \times 4 \times \cancel{25}} {\cancel{25}} \\\\
                                            &= 7 \times 4 \\\\
                                            &= 28\%
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$28\%$

</div>

                                            &= \dfrac {7 \times 100} {25} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {7 \times 4 \times 25} {25} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {7 \times 4 \times \cancel{25}} {\cancel{25}} \\\\
                                            &= 7 \times 4 \\\\
                                            &= 28\%
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the **maximum** number of matchboxes that could fit, in one layer, onto a tray that is 
$20 \ \text{cm}$ long and $15 \ \text{cm}$ wide?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$


As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of Matchboxes}    &= \dfrac {\text {Area of tray}} {\text {Area of the base of matchbox}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 15} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 3 \times 5} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
                                &= 20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_rectangle_expanded

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of Matchboxes}    &= \dfrac {\text {Area of tray}} {\text {Area of the base of matchbox}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 15} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 3 \times 5} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
                                &= 20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the maximum number of matchboxes that could be fitted into a box measuring $18 \ \text{cm}$ by $25 \ \text{cm}$ by $10 \ \text{cm}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Volume} = \text{length} \times \text{width} \times \text{height}$


As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of matchboxes}            &= \dfrac {\text {Volume of box}} {\text {Volume of a matchbox}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {18 \times 25 \times 10} {1 \times 3 \times 5} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 6 \times 5 \times 5 \times 10} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 6 \times \cancel {5} \times 5 \times 10} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
                                        &= 6 \times 5 \times 10 \\\\
                                        &= 300
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$300$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_katex_formula_volume_cuboid

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of matchboxes}            &= \dfrac {\text {Volume of box}} {\text {Volume of a matchbox}} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {18 \times 25 \times 10} {1 \times 3 \times 5} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {3 \times 6 \times 5 \times 5 \times 10} {3 \times 5} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 6 \times \cancel {5} \times 5 \times 10} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
                                        &= 6 \times 5 \times 10 \\\\
                                        &= 300
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$300$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4A4E7B81-270C-40B5-B238-65BE74C68BA8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

In order to convert from the imperial units ounces and pounds to the metric unit kilograms, you should use the following conversions:

$
\begin{aligned}
16 \ \text{ounces} &= 1 \ \text{pound}\\
2.2 \ \text{pounds} &= 1 \ \text{kg}
\end{aligned}
$

Newborn tiger cubs weigh about $56 \ \text{ounces}$.

Circle the mass in kilograms which gives the best approximation of the mass of a newborn tiger cub.

$0.5 \ \text{kg} \quad 1 \ \text{kg} \quad 1.5 \ \text{kg} \quad 2 \ \text{kg} \quad 2.5 \ \text{kg} \quad 3 \ \text{kg}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 56 \ \text{ounces} \\\\
&= (56 \div 16) \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= 3.5 \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= (3.5 \div 2.2) \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.59 \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.5 \ \text{kg} \ \text{(approx.)} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
<div class='working'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

1.5 kg

</div>
<div class='answer'>

TODOANSWER

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 56 \ \text{ounces} \\\\
&= (56 \div 16) \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= 3.5 \ \text{pounds} \\\\
&= (3.5 \div 2.2) \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.59 \ \text{kg} \\\\
&= 1.5 \ \text{kg} \ \text{(approx.)} \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>TODOWORKING
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>1.5 kg
</code></pre>

<div class='question question'>

A bird has $2$ legs, a cat has $4$ legs, an insect has $6$ legs and a spider has $8$ legs.

Claire looks at some animals and counts all their legs.

She counts $38$ legs.

There are twice as many birds as spiders and twice as many cats as insects.

How many of each type of animal can she see?
